# Graph Report - agentshroud  (2026-10-18)

## Corpus Check
- 20 files · ~2,454,883 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 33433 nodes · 55335 edges · 2034 communities (1339 shown, 695 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 86% EXTRACTED · 14% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7936 edges (avg confidence: 0.57)
- Token cost: 22,000 input · 6,000 output
